## MODEL ✨

I've moved the **Clear History** functionality into the native navigation header within `app/(tabs)/chat/_layout.tsx`.

### What changed:
1.  **Layout Refactor**: Moved the `deleteAllMessages` mutation, the `confirmDeleteAll` function, and the `AlertDialog` component into the layout. This allows the button to live in the native top-right `headerRight` position.
2.  **Native feel**: By using the navigation header, the trash icon now appears alongside the "Roe Bot" title in a standard platform-consistent way.
3.  **Code Cleanup**: Removed the redundant manual header and delete logic from `app/(tabs)/chat/index.tsx`. The screen now focuses entirely on the chat messages and input.
4.  **Optimized Sync**: The layout now invalidates the chat cache upon deletion, ensuring the screen refreshes to the empty state immediately.

This gives the chat a much cleaner, more native look while keeping the code organized and efficient.

similar\n      processedMessages = rawData.map((msg: any) => ({\n        ...msg,\n        files:\n          msg.files ||\n          msg.MessageFile ||\n          msg.messageFiles ||\n          msg.attachments ||\n          [],\n      }));\n    }\n\n    return [...processedMessages, ...optimisticMessages];\n  }, [serverMessages, optimisticMessages]);\n\n  const ensureLocalFile = async (uri: string, filename: string) => {\n    if (Platform.OS === \"web\") return uri;\n    try {\n      const cacheDir = (FileSystem as any).cacheDirectory;\n      const localUri = `${cacheDir}${Date.now()}_${filename}`;\n      await FileSystem.copyAsync({ from: uri, to: localUri });\n      return localUri;\n    } catch (e) {\n      return uri;\n    }\n  };\n\n  const pickImage = async () => {\n    const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({\n      mediaTypes: [\"images\"],\n      quality: 0.7,\n      base64: true,\n    });\n\n    if (!result.canceled) {\n      const asset = result.assets[0];\n      const localUri = await ensureLocalFile(\n        asset.uri,\n        asset.fileName || \"image.jpg\",\n      );\n      setSelectedFiles((prev) => [\n        ...prev,\n        {\n          uri: localUri,\n          name: asset.fileName || \"image.jpg\",\n          type: \"image\",\n          mimeType: asset.mimeType || \"image/jpeg\",\n          base64: asset.base64 || undefined,\n        },\n      ]);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const pickDocument = async () => {\n    const result = await DocumentPicker.getDocumentAsync({\n      type: \"*/*\",\n      copyToCacheDirectory: true,\n    });\n\n    if (!result.canceled) {\n      const asset = result.assets[0];\n      const localUri = await ensureLocalFile(asset.uri, asset.name);\n      let base64: string | undefined;\n      try {\n        base64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(localUri, {\n          encoding: \"base64\",\n        });\n      } catch (e) {}\n\n      setSelectedFiles((prev) => [\n        ...prev,\n        {\n          uri: localUri,\n          name: asset.name,\n          type: \"document\",\n          mimeType: asset.mimeType || \"application/octet-stream\",\n          base64,\n        },\n      ]);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const removeFile = (index: number) => {\n    setSelectedFiles((prev) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));\n  };\n\n  const sendMessage = async (textOverride?: string) => {\n    const messageText = (\n      typeof textOverride === \"string\" ? textOverride : input\n    ).trim();\n    if (\n      (!messageText && selectedFiles.length === 0) ||\n      !sessionId ||\n      sendMessageMutation.isPending\n    )\n      return;\n\n    const file = selectedFiles[0];\n\n    // Create optimistic message\n    const optimisticMsg: Message = {\n      id: `temp-${Date.now()}`,\n      role: \"USER\",\n      content: messageText || \"Analyze this file\",\n      createdAt: new Date(),\n      files: file\n        ? [{ id: \"temp-file\", url: file.uri, mimeType: file.mimeType }]\n        : [],\n    };\n\n    setOptimisticMessages((prev) => [...prev, optimisticMsg]);\n    setInput(\"\");\n    setSelectedFiles([]);\n\n    try {\n      await sendMessageMutation.mutateAsync({\n        sessionId,\n        content: messageText || \"Analyze this file\",\n        file: file\n          ? {\n              base64: file.base64!,\n              mimeType: file.mimeType,\n            }\n          : undefined,\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      setOptimisticMessages((prev) =>\n        prev.filter((m) => m.id !== optimisticMsg.id),\n      );\n      console.error(\"Failed to send message\", e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderMessage = ({ item }: { item: Message }) => {\n    const isUser = item.role === \"USER\";\n\n    // Support multiple possible property names for files from backend (Prisma relations)\n    const messageFiles =\n      item.files ||\n      (item as any).MessageFile ||\n      (item as any).messageFiles ||\n      (item as any).attachments;\n\n    console.log(\"Message: \", messageFiles);\n\n    return (\n      <View\n        key={item.id}\n        style={[\n          styles.messageContainer,\n          isUser ? styles.userMessage : styles.botMessage,\n          { backgroundColor: isUser ? accent : muted },\n        ]}\n      >\n        <View style={styles.messageHeader}>\n          {isUser ? (\n            <User size={12} color=\"#fff\" />\n          ) : (\n            <Bot size={12} color={text} />\n          )}\n          <Text\n            style={{\n              fontSize: 10,\n              marginLeft: 4,\n              color: isUser ? \"#fff\" : text,\n              fontWeight: \"600\",\n              opacity: 0.8,\n            }}\n          >\n            {isUser ? \"You\" : \"Roe Bot\"}\n          </Text>\n        </View>\n\n        {messageFiles?.map((file: any) => {\n          // Handle both server base64 data and local optimistic URIs\n          // Support multiple field names like 'url', 'data', or 'uri'\n          let imageUri = file.url || file.data || file.uri;\n\n          if (!imageUri) return null;\n\n          // Check if it's already a full URI (web URL, local file, or data URI)\n          const isFullUri =\n            imageUri.startsWith(\"http\") ||\n            imageUri.startsWith(\"file://\") ||\n            imageUri.startsWith(\"content://\") ||\n            imageUri.startsWith(\"data:\");\n\n          if (!isFullUri) {\n            imageUri = `data:${file.mimeType};base64,${imageUri}`;\n          }\n\n          return (\n            <View key={file.id || Math.random()} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>\n              {file.mimeType.startsWith(\"image\") ? (\n                <Image\n                  source={{ uri: imageUri }}\n                  style={{ width: \"100%\", height: 150, borderRadius: 12 }}\n                  resizeMode=\"cover\"\n                />\n              ) : (\n                <View style={styles.filePlaceholder}>\n                  <FileText size={16} color={isUser ? \"#fff\" : text} />\n                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 12, color: isUser ? \"#fff\" : text }}>\n                    Document\n                  </Text>\n                </View>\n              )}\n            </View>\n          );\n        })}\n\n        {isUser ? (\n          <Text style={{ color: \"#fff\", fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 20 }}>\n            {item.content}\n          </Text>\n        ) : (\n          <Markdown\n            style={{\n              body: { color: text, fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 22 },\n              paragraph: { marginTop: 0, marginBottom: 8 },\n            }}\n          >\n            {item.content}\n          </Markdown>\n        )}\n      </View>\n    );\n  };\n\n  const isLoading =\n    authLoading || historyLoading || createSessionMutation.isPending;\n\n  return (\n    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: background }}>\n      <Animated.View style={{ flex: 1, paddingBottom }}>\n        <FlatList\n          ref={flatListRef}\n          data={messages}\n          renderItem={renderMessage}\n          keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}\n          contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, paddingBottom: 32, gap: 16 }}\n          style={{ flex: 1 }}\n          onContentSizeChange={() =>\n            flatListRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true })\n          }\n          ListEmptyComponent={\n            !isLoading ? (\n              <View style={styles.emptyState}>\n                <Bot size={64} color={text} />\n                <Text style={styles.emptyText}>\n                  Assalamu'alaikum!{\"\\n\"}How can I help you today?\n                </Text>\n              </View>\n            ) : null\n          }\n        />\n\n        {(sendMessageMutation.isPending || isLoading) && (\n          <View style={styles.loadingContainer}>\n            <Spinner size=\"sm\" />\n            <Text variant=\"caption\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n              {sendMessageMutation.isPending\n                ? \"Roe is thinking...\"\n                : \"Connecting...\"}\n            </Text>\n          </View>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Selected Files Preview */}\n        {selectedFiles.length > 0 && (\n          <View style={styles.previewContainer}>\n            {selectedFiles.map((file, idx) => (\n              <View key={idx} style={styles.previewBox}>\n                {file.type === \"image\" ? (\n                  <Image\n                    source={{ uri: file.uri }}\n                    style={styles.previewImage}\n                  />\n                ) : (\n                  <FileText size={20} color={text} />\n                )}\n                <TouchableOpacity\n                  onPress={() => removeFile(idx)}\n                  style={styles.removeBtn}\n                >\n                  <X size={12} color=\"#fff\" />\n                </TouchableOpacity>\n              </View>\n            ))}\n          </View>\n        )}\n\n        {userId && (\n          <View\n            style={[\n              styles.inputContainer,\n              { borderTopColor: border, backgroundColor: background },\n            ]}\n          >\n            <TouchableOpacity onPress={pickImage} style={{ padding: 4 }}>\n              <ImageIcon size={22} color={text} style={{ opacity: 0.6 }} />\n            </TouchableOpacity>\n\n            <TouchableOpacity onPress={pickDocument} style={{ padding: 4 }}>\n              <Paperclip size={22} color={text} style={{ opacity: 0.6 }} />\n            </TouchableOpacity>\n\n            <Input\n              value={input}\n              onChangeText={setInput}\n              placeholder=\"Ask Roe...\"\n              containerStyle={{ flex: 1 }}\n              inputStyle={{ height: 44 }}\n              onSubmitEditing={(e: any) => sendMessage(e.nativeEvent.text)}\n              blurOnSubmit={false}\n            />\n\n            <TouchableOpacity\n              onPress={() => sendMessage()}\n              disabled={\n                (!input.trim() && selectedFiles.length === 0) ||\n                sendMessageMutation.isPending\n              }\n              style={[\n                styles.sendBtn,\n                {\n                  backgroundColor:\n                    input.trim() || selectedFiles.length > 0 ? accent : muted,\n                },\n              ]}\n            >\n              {sendMessageMutation.isPending ? (\n                <Spinner size=\"sm\" />\n              ) : (\n                <Send\n                  size={20}\n                  color={\n                    input.trim() || selectedFiles.length > 0 ? \"#fff\" : text\n                  }\n                />\n              )}\n            </TouchableOpacity>\n          </View>\n        )}\n      </Animated.View>\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  messageContainer: {\n    padding: 12,\n    paddingHorizontal: 16,\n    borderRadius: 20,\n    maxWidth: \"85%\",\n  },\n  userMessage: { alignSelf: \"flex-end\", borderBottomRightRadius: 4 },\n  botMessage: { alignSelf: \"flex-start\", borderBottomLeftRadius: 4 },\n  messageHeader: {\n    flexDirection: \"row\",\n    alignItems: \"center\",\n    marginBottom: 6,\n  },\n  filePlaceholder: {\n    flexDirection: \"row\",\n    alignItems: \"center\",\n    backgroundColor: \"rgba(0,0,0,0.1)\",\n    padding: 8,\n    borderRadius: 8,\n    gap: 8,\n  },\n  emptyState: {\n    flex: 1,\n    alignItems: \"center\",\n    justifyContent: \"center\",\n    marginTop: 100,\n    opacity: 0.3,\n  },\n  emptyText: { marginTop: 16, textAlign: \"center\", fontWeight: \"500\" },\n  loadingContainer: {\n    paddingHorizontal: 16,\n    paddingVertical: 8,\n    flexDirection: \"row\",\n    alignItems: \"center\",\n    gap: 8,\n  },\n  previewContainer: {\n    paddingHorizontal: 16,\n    paddingVertical: 8,\n    flexDirection: \"row\",\n    gap: 8,\n    flexWrap: \"wrap\",\n  },\n  previewBox: {\n    width: 60,\n    height: 60,\n    borderRadius: 8,\n ## MODEL ✨

I've fixed the base64 detection logic to ensure images from the database are displayed correctly.

The issue was that my previous check was too simple and incorrectly identified raw base64 data (which often contains slashes) as a full URI. This prevented the app from adding the necessary `data:image/jpeg;base64,...` prefix.

### Improvements:
*   **Robust Detection**: The app now explicitly checks for valid URI prefixes like `http`, `file://`, `content://`, or `data:`. If the image data doesn't start with one of these, it correctly treats it as raw base64 and prepends the required data URI metadata.
*   **Rendering Fix**: This ensures that strings starting with `/9j/` (standard for base64 JPEGs) are properly formatted before being passed to the React Native `Image` component.

Your chat history should now render all previously sent images properly!

They are not uploaded to Google's cloud servers in a way that makes them accessible via the standard Gemini web interface.\\n2.  **Infrastructure Differences:** The CLI typically interacts with the Gemini API (via Google AI Studio or Vertex AI) or the Gemini Code Assist API, which operate independently from the consumer-facing Gemini website.\\n3.  **Project Context:** CLI sessions are \\\"project-scoped,\\\" meaning they are tied to the specific directory and codebase you are working in, including local file context that the website would not have access to.\\n\\n### Workaround: Exporting Conversations\\nIf you need to view or share your CLI conversation in a browser-friendly format, you can export it to a file:\\n- Use the command **`/chat share [filename].md`** (or `/resume share`) to export the current conversation to a Markdown file. \\n- You can then open this Markdown file in any browser or editor, or copy-paste the content into the Gemini website if you wish to start a new thread there using that context.\\n\\n*Note: My investigation was interrupted due to technical limits, but I have reviewed the core session management and command documentation to provide this answer.*\",\n  \"sources\": [\n    \"cli/session-management.md\",\n    \"reference/commands.md\",\n    \"resources/faq.md\"\n  ]\n}"

## MODEL ✨

It is **not currently possible** to directly sync or continue your Gemini CLI session on the Gemini website (gemini.google.com). 

This is because the CLI stores your sessions locally on your machine and operates within your local project context, whereas the website uses a separate cloud-based infrastructure.

### Recommended Workaround: Export to Markdown
You can export this conversation to a file and then copy-paste it into the Gemini website if needed:

1.  In your terminal, use the command:
    ` /chat share conversation.md`
2.  Open `conversation.md` in any text editor.
3.  Copy the content you want and paste it into a new thread on the Gemini website.

This allows you to take the context of our current work with you to the web interface.
